# Sourced Roles — Chad Butler

**Compiled:** 2026-08-02 · **Evidence gathered:** 2026-08-01 · **Roles listed:** 94 · **Documented exclusions:** 329 · **Unverified leads:** 242

**Coverage:** 7 sourcing waves · 45 sourcing + verification agents · ~500 companies screened across wealthtech, fintech, compliance and insurance tech, financial data, asset-management distribution, pharma, biotech, medical device, diagnostics, animal health and dental, healthcare IT, banking technology, CFO-office and HR SaaS, FSI enterprise software, energy and power, building technology, industrial manufacturing, power trading and grid, federal agencies, and non-sales surveillance, compliance consulting, and research.

---

## ⚠️ READ THIS FIRST — evidence standard

**No role in this file was verified by loading its live posting.** This session's network policy blocked outbound connections to every job board and career site — the egress proxy returned `403 policy denial` on CONNECT to Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, Workday, USAJOBS, and every employer careers domain tested (the block was universal; even `example.com` failed). Web search remained available, so agents found roles and pulled posting text from the search index, but **nobody clicked through to a live page.**

What that means for you:

- **Every quote, salary, and requirement below came from search-indexed copies of the posting text**, retrieved 2026-08-01, with the source named on each entry's `Verification` line. Cross-checked across sources where possible.
- **Postings may have closed.** Index snapshots lag reality by days to weeks.
- **Verify before investing effort.** Open the URL, confirm it's live, and re-read the degree and license lines yourself — those two screens kill most of your matches, and index text is exactly where a stale or partial quote would hide.
- The original brief's rule stands: nothing here is marked "verified live," because none of it is.

To get true verification, this environment needs a network policy that allows job-board access — the sweep results are cached, so re-running only the verification stage would be quick.

---

## Screening criteria applied (as updated through the session)

| Rule | Status |
|---|---|
| **No securities licenses** | Hard kill. Series 6/7/24/63/65/66 required, or "obtain within X days" → excluded with the quoted line. **Industry no longer restricted** — only the license line disqualifies. |
| **Remote or field-based** | Hard requirement, so the RIA keeps running. Remote must include Utah; field/territory roles must be home-based. **All office-based onsite/hybrid roles excluded, including Utah offices.** |
| **No Utah-HQ companies** | Hard kill — retired the entire original Silicon Slopes lane (27 companies) plus Jump, Recursion, Merit Medical, ARUP, Myriad, BioFire, Health Catalyst, Atomic, Vanilla. |
| **Sales not required** | Research, surveillance, compliance, analysis, consulting all in scope as of the final update. |
| **No call-center/phone-queue work** | Hard kill — monitoring and investigation desks are fine, phone queues are not. |
| **Degree** | Finance/SaaS: hard "Bachelor's required" → excluded. Pharma/device/energy/research: labeled and ranked lower, not excluded. Every role carries its exact degree quote. |
| **Seniority** | 5+ years. No SDR/BDR, no entry-level. |
| **Comp** | Wealthtech/fintech $150K+ base preferred; pharma/device/other $120K+; non-sales ~$110K+. Never estimated — "not disclosed" where absent. |
| **Excluded employers** | **Merck (all divisions — including Verona Pharma, acquired Oct 2025)** and Human Interest. Already-applied roles (Advisor360°, Addepar) not re-sourced. |

These are AI-training subject-matter-expert engagements — flexible hours, no license, and your domain depth is the whole product.

## Answers to your two direct questions

**Remote monitoring jobs — are they a thing?** Yes, but the well-paid version isn't the one you were probably picturing. Physical/video/alarm monitoring (GSOC operators, remote guarding) is real and genuinely remote, but it's hourly shift work — documented kills in this file run **$18–30/hour**, far under your floor. The version that pays is **desk-based surveillance and financial-crime monitoring**: trade surveillance, sanctions screening, AML/FIU investigation. That corner wants exactly your background, doesn't require active registration, and pays $108K–$227K in the roles below. Wave W1 is that answer.

**Federal remote roles:** zero qualifying announcements, and it's structural, not a thin search. After the January 2025 return-to-office order these agencies have essentially stopped posting public "Remote job: Yes" announcements — everything live is duty-station-plus-telework, internal-merit-promotion-only, or genuinely remote but closed months ago. Forty-three documented kills are in the Excluded section, including the one that got away: **FHFA Examiner (0570), remote with location negotiable, SLC allowed, no degree requirement — closed 05/11/2026.** Set a USAJOBS saved search for FHFA 0570 reposts; that's the single best federal target for your profile.

## Federal / USAJOBS — Lanes F1–F3


**No qualifying roles this wave.** All three shards (F1 financial regulators, F2 retirement/benefits/business-facing agencies, F3 general USAJOBS remote sweep) returned zero includable announcements — an honest empty set, not a thin search. As of 2026-08-01, after the January 2025 federal return-to-office order, these agencies have essentially stopped posting "Remote job: Yes" announcements open to the public: everything live is either duty-station+telework (SEC, OCC, FinCEN, DOL, IRS), internal-only (OCC, NCUA, FDIC, USDA RD, USCIS, FCC, ASC, VBA), or genuinely remote but closed in May–July 2026. The strongest live leads sit in the Could-Not-Verify section below because one decisive field (the USAJOBS "Remote job" flag or hiring path) could not be read through the blocked proxy — several are time-sensitive and worth a 30-second human check.

# Excluded

_Every notable role screened out, with the specific disqualifier quoted. This section exists so the screening can be audited — if a kill looks wrong, it can be challenged against the quoted language._

## Wealth technology & fintech distribution — Lanes A, B


*All disqualifier quotes below were captured from search-index snippets or job-board mirrors of the actual postings (careers.ta.com, Ladders, ZipRecruiter, Built In, etc.), not live pages — the wave had no working fetch channel. Kills rest on mirror evidence; judgment-call flags are preserved.*

**License kills**
- **Flourish (MassMutual) — Business Development Manager:** must "either have a Series 7 license or be willing to get a Series 7 within 90 days of joining, and either have a Series 63 license or be willing to get a Series 63 within 90 days of joining" plus "ability to pass a fingerprint background check to qualify as a fingerprinted person under FINRA." Also NYC-based. (job-boards.greenhouse.io/flourish/jobs/5620333004)
- **Flourish (MassMutual) — Director of Business Development:** "Either have a Series 7 or willingness to get a Series 7 within 60 days of joining." Eligible geography is "the western half of the US including Texas, Colorado, Arizona or California" (Utah not named). (job-boards.greenhouse.io/flourish/jobs/6014491004)
- **SEI — Business Development Director (Independent Broker-Dealer channel):** double kill — "Current NASAA Series 65 or Series 66 license or ability to acquire within 90 days" plus required "Bachelor's degree in Business, Finance, Economics or in a related field." Oaks, PA on the company posting (careers.seic.com/global/en/job/R0036013); Built In mirror shows remote at $100-120K.
- **Empower — Regional Sales Director (Greenwood Village / multiple):** "FINRA Series 6, 63 and applicable state insurance licenses" plus required "Bachelor's degree in Business or related field." (jobs.empower.com/job/greenwood-village/regional-sales-director/42743/81056544192)
- **LeafHouse — Sales Director (Austin, TX):** "FINRA licensing required including series 65 within 90 days of employment." Degree line was acceptable ("Bachelor's degree or three years of equivalent experience"), but license kills it; also Austin-based with no remote designation, 3-5 yrs skews junior. (JazzHR: leafhouse.applytojob.com)
- **iCapital (SIMON) — Regional Director - Structured Investments Distribution - Vice President:** "Series 7 and 63 licenses are required." Otherwise adjacent to Chad's background — license is the sole but fatal screen. (job-boards.greenhouse.io/icapitalnetwork)
- **iCapital (SIMON) — Structured Investments Platform Sales - Vice President / Senior Vice President:** "The position requires holding series 7 and 63 licenses."
- **DPL Financial Partners — Senior Membership Sales Consultant (Louisville, KY):** triple kill — "Bachelor's Degree in business or related field required"; "A Variable and Life Insurance License is required" (variable licensing entails FINRA registration); Louisville-based, not remote, not Utah.
- **Carson Group — VP, Business Development (Channel Partnerships), Remote $151,000–$230,000 base:** "Active Series 65, or Series 7 & 66, or advanced designation (CFP®, CFA®, ChFC®, etc.) is required." Chad's 7/63/66 are lapsed and he holds no listed designation. JUDGMENT-CALL FLAG: otherwise the best-fit role in shard B2 (degree line was "Bachelor's degree or equivalent professional experience," comp in range, remote, RIA-aggregator channel); quote is from a job-board mirror — worth re-checking if a CFP/designation path is ever pursued.
- **CAIS — Business Development roles (multiple, incl. Enterprise Business Development Associate):** BD roles "requires Series 7 & 63 licenses" and other positions "require Series 7 or willingness to obtain within 6 months." The one role without a license line (Enterprise BD Associate, Austin) is SDR-adjacent (~2 yrs) and onsite Austin — too junior.
- **Orion Advisor Solutions — Business Development Consultant OAT (R2323):** "requires a Series 65 license or both Series 7 and 66 licenses, and if the required license(s) are not held at the time of hire, they must be obtained within 90 days of the start date"; also territory-restricted — "candidates must live in the NE territory: CT, DE, MD, MA, NJ, NY, PA, or RI" — and only 3 years field-sales experience required (junior). (Quoted via careers.ta.com mirror.)
- **Orion Advisor Solutions — Senior Advisor Consultant (R2216, Greater Omaha Area):** candidates "must have a current Investment Adviser Representative license (Series 65 or Series 7 & 66)" — CURRENT license required, Chad's are inactive; territory "External candidates must live in the North Central territory (CO, KS, WI, MO, NE, SD)." Otherwise the closest Orion match to Chad's wholesaling background. (careers.ta.com mirror.)
- **Orion Advisor Solutions — Internal Sales Consultant (R2064):** "Current Series 7 and 66 (or 63&65) licenses are required or there must be the ability to obtain within 90 days of employment"; also internal/inside sales, ~2-year tier (seniority fail). Its in-office rotation includes Provo, UT (3 days/week) — under the updated rules that office rotation would also exclude it.
- **Orion Advisor Solutions — Business Development Consultant (TA board id 71948479, Orion Portfolio Solutions/Brinker/OCIO):** triple kill — hard degree "minimum of a Bachelor of Science in Business Administration, Finance or related field"; Series 65 or 7+66 "must be obtained within 90 days of the start date" if not held; junior inbound role ("receiving incoming calls," minimum 2 years inside sales). (careers.ta.com mirror.)

## Federal / USAJOBS — Lanes F1–F3


#### Not-remote (telework-with-duty-station = kill)

- **SEC — Division of Examinations (Office of Investment Advisers and Investment Companies, Private Funds Unit, Office of Clearance and Settlement) — Securities Compliance Examiner, SK-1831, Announcement 26-DE-13005297-CSS (control 877996200), closes 08/07/2026:** NOT REMOTE (hard constraint 1). Announcement text: "the selectee is required to report to the duty station(s) listed" and "Total salary (base pay + locality) is dependent upon duty location" — a multi-city duty-station posting that is telework-eligible under SEC policy, not a remote job. Otherwise this is the bullseye: open to the public ("U.S. Citizens, Nationals or those who owe allegiance to the U.S."), 1831 series (qualifies via specialized experience in "interpreting and applying the provisions of the Investment Advisers Act and/or the Investment Company Act... and performing examinations... of securities-related financial institutions" — no degree wall), travel ~5 days/month, LIVE and closing 08/07/2026. **IMPORTANT — URGENT by 08/07:** the SEC operates a Salt Lake City Regional Office, and comparable EXAMS postings have listed "Salt Lake City, UT" among duty locations — Chad should open https://sec.usajobs.gov/job/877996200 and check the duty-location list; if SLC is on it, this fails only the remote screen, not a Chad-can't-do-it screen (his single strongest federal target from home turf — the IA/IC + Private Funds Unit is the exact program that examines firms like his). Conflict-of-interest: "Financial disclosure is required" — as owner of Harvest Lane Investment Partners (an RIA regulated by the exam program he'd join), he would face SEC ethics restrictions and would very likely need to divest/wind down the firm. ## Power trading, grid & energy market data — Lanes Z1–Z3


**Utah-HQ rule (hard constraint — human can override)**
- **WECC (Western Electricity Coordinating Council) — Vice President, Strategic Engagement (remote-eligible):** UTAH-HQ RULE (hard constraint 2) — WECC is Salt Lake City-headquartered; logged explicitly for human override because it is otherwise an outstanding profile match: remote-eligible, "requires 15+ years in business leadership with expertise in strategic communications and stakeholder relations," leads communications/training/outreach/external affairs, "develop high quality relationships with industry stakeholders and policymakers" (per Vaia-indexed posting, 2026; also listed at wecc.org/careers). For a Sandy, UT candidate this may even be a feature, not a bug — recommend human review. Shard Z3 notes flag it as "arguably the single best profile match found in this shard — the SLC HQ is only a rule-kill, not a candidate problem."

Stealth's operator postings are monitoring-center-based (Dallas TX etc.), not remote.
- **Deep Sentinel — Live Sentinel Guard / Surveillance Agent (work-from-home):** MONITORING REALITY-CHECK KILL #2 — genuinely remote, but: "the hourly range for one position is $25 - $30 per hour"; "Live Monitoring Agents with background in Surveillance are listed at $15 Per Hour"; employee-reported "$22.00 an hour for watching cameras". Best case $30/hr ≈ $62K/yr — far below the $90-110K floor, junior camera-watching.
- **Securitas — Alarm Monitoring Operator (24/7 Alarm Monitoring Center):** MONITORING REALITY-CHECK KILL #3 — ZipRecruiter (May 2026): Securitas average "$17.15/hr, range $11.30 to $21.88"; broader remote-alarm-monitoring category average "$22.21/hr, most between $18.27 and $25.00". Postings are center-based "Mid-Shift, 2nd, or 3rd shift" — shift work is acceptable but onsite centers are not, and $18-25/hr fails the floor regardless.
- **GSOC / command-center category (incl. ADT-type alarm and WFH GSOC postings) — Remote GSOC Operator; Command Center Operations Manager:** MONITORING REALITY-CHECK CONCLUSION — operator level: WFH GSOC operator average "$19.68/hr, most between $16.54 and $22.12" (ZipRecruiter, Jul 25, 2026). Management level: command center operations manager average "$73,687/yr, most between $52,500 and $91,000" (ZipRecruiter, May 23, 2026); the rare $100-130K or "$41.97-$65.86/hr" manager postings found are onsite command centers and/or require a physical-security/GSOC career track Chad does not have.

Even the broader "remote NOC" 75th percentile ($103,500) misses the $110K floor, and these roles require hands-on networking/IT certs he does not have.

- **Financial-services mystery shopping (Pinnacle, Sinclair, IntelliShop, Market Force) — bank/credit-union mystery shopper (GIG):** Gig-scale money: simple bank visits pay "$10 to $25", detailed assignments "$75 to $150 or more" per shop; Sinclair "typically $8 to $25 plus reimbursement". Per-task gig income, no path to the comp floor; documented so the base is covered.
- **FINRA Dispute Resolution — Arbitrator roster (case-based, not employment):** Honoraria ~$600 per hearing session ($725 chair) — BUT per FINRA.org, effective May 24, 2025 FINRA now requires new arbitrator applicants to hold "a four-year college degree and at least five years of full-time paid professional work experience"; the old standard (5 yrs work + 2 yrs college credits) would have qualified Chad, the new one likely does not — verify directly with FINRA Dispute Resolution before spending time.

## Federal / USAJOBS — Lanes F1–F3


*Every direct page load of usajobs.gov (and sec.usajobs.gov, cfpb.usajobs.gov, gsa.usajobs.gov, data.usajobs.gov) — plus mirror sites (federalgovernmentjobs.us, employeebenefitsjobs.com, careerbuilder detail pages, r.jina.ai, archive.org) — returned HTTP 403 through the session egress proxy, a policy denial the proxy README instructs not to work around; the USAJOBS API requires a key (registration prohibited by task rules). Evidence standard used instead: all facts were assembled from search-engine-indexed copies of the live announcement pages, cross-checked across multiple queries. Quotes are real indexed page text but could not be confirmed against live pages. The one field that rarely surfaces in indexed copies is the literal "Remote job: Yes/No" toggle — which is exactly why the live leads below need a 30-second human check of the announcement's Overview box.*

Gathered: LIVE and otherwise qualifying — open to the public ("U.S. citizens or U.S. Nationals; or CTAP/ICTAP eligibles; no prior Federal experience is required"), GS-14 ceiling $187,093, stakeholder-engagement duties ("developing long-term plans and goals to build relationships with regulated financial institutions, law enforcement agencies, and other national security agencies") that map directly onto his 15 years of institutional relationship-building — but the location/remote field could not be confirmed: one indexed reading called it "a remote position," while CareerBuilder's scrape of the same announcement lists the duty location as Washington, DC and the announcement offers only "opportunities for telework and flexible work schedules." He should open the announcement and check the Location box; include only if it reads "Anywhere in the U.S. (remote job)" with no Utah exclusion. AML/CFT subject matter is adjacent to, not identical with, his securities background — an honest stretch, not a lay-up. ## Power trading, grid & energy market data — Lanes Z1–Z3

# Audit notes

_From the completeness critic that reviewed the main sweep's compiled output._


- **critical** — Zero included roles across all shards: every verification session had a total egress block (403 on all hosts incl. example.com) plus an exhausted shared WebSearch budget; the deliverable is a re-run queue of ~60 unverified leads, not a vetted job list, and must be re-run with working egress before going to the candidate.
- **critical** — Lanes C (~47 pharma companies), D (29 device/diagnostics companies), and E (~28 Utah SaaS companies) received ZERO screening — not even discovery ran (budget drained by earlier shards); these are the degree-relaxed lanes and likely the most accessible segment; empty excluded[] lists there carry no market signal.
- **critical** — The reverify pass confirmed liveness of NONE of the 14 prior pipeline roles, and 4+ show active closure/retitle risk (FMG Enterprise AE absent from index, Wealthbox Enterprise AE absent, Gridline apparently retitled to "Director, RIA Partnerships", Envestnet posting family window closed 2026-07-31) — applying from the old list risks dead or downgraded reqs.
- **moderate** — No disqualification quote anywhere comes from a live posting: every license/degree/geography quote is from search-index snippets or mirrors (careers.ta.com, Ladders, ZipRecruiter, Built In); kills are probably safe but the candidate must be told all exclusions rest on mirror evidence.
- **moderate** — Cross-shard contradiction: Apex Fintech "Sales Director, Advisory" excluded in A2 (hybrid NJ/Austin) but listed as B2's #2 priority prospect with remote status unknown — reconciled in this fragment as EXCLUDED (office-hybrid) with the conflict noted.
- **moderate** — Internal contradiction on Envestnet BD Director – RIA (17857841): A2 parks it as a top re-check while reverify reports its application window closed 2026-07-31 — closure flagged prominently here (kept in CNV, not ROLES).
- **moderate** — ~9 leads carry aggregator URLs instead of direct employer/ATS links (iCapital West RD via themuse.com, Apex via builtin.com, Broadridge via builtinnyc.com, Nitrogen via a Glassdoor company page, Clearwater via Glassdoor, Enfusion via purpose.jobs, Arcesium via startup.jobs, InvestCloud via builtinla.com, eMoney Advisory ESE via mediabistro.com).
- **moderate** — Trust & Will Sr AE II (the best functional fit in lane A2, $250-300K remote) was excluded on a state-eligibility list quoted from the careers page and a SIBLING posting, not the req itself (trustwill/jobs/4110450009 never loaded) — false-negative risk on the strongest finance-lane role.
- **moderate** — Zinnia VP Partnerships & Alliances ($270-300K remote; Utah IS an eligible Zinnia remote state) was excluded on a mirror-sourced hard-degree quote; if the live line reads "preferred" it becomes the #1 role in the lane — exclusion should be treated as provisional.
- **moderate** — Practifi Account Manager retained as a lead despite being a Client-Success-labeled post-sale role, inconsistent with the GeoWealth exclusion for the same pattern — resolve (both leads or both kills) before delivery; Central/Eastern TZ preference is an added soft negative.
- **minor** — Duplicate exclusion: Betterment "Senior Manager of Advised 401k Sales" appeared in both A1 and B1 (deduplicated in this fragment).
- **minor** — Unresolved eMoney dedupe cluster: Jobvite req oq7RxfwF, sibling req oey5vfw6 (identical title), and the Mediabistro-sourced "Advisory Enterprise Sales Executive - Growth Accounts" may be one, two, or three distinct roles — the candidate could triple-count one opportunity.
- **minor** — One comp estimate slipped in against the never-estimate rule: Kestra M&A BD Specialist "est. $80K-$120K" — should be treated as "not disclosed" (role dies on seniority/geography regardless).
- **minor** — Lane E contained a bookkeeping row masquerading as a company ("Lendio (Lehi) — duplicate guard") — removed in this fragment.
- **minor** — URL plausibility could not be verified live from any session (the audit session was also fully egress-blocked); formats are structurally consistent with the claimed ATS platforms so nothing looks fabricated, but every URL — including the priority click-through list — is unconfirmed.
